<script>
  (function () {
    const playBtn = document.getElementById('voice-play-btn');
    const playIcon = document.getElementById('voice-play-icon');
    let isPlaying = false;

    if (playBtn && playIcon) {
      playBtn.addEventListener('click', function () {
        isPlaying = !isPlaying;
        playIcon.textContent = isPlaying ? 'pause' : 'play_arrow';
      });
    }

    const micBtn = document.getElementById('mic-btn');
    let isRecording = false;
    if (micBtn) {
      micBtn.addEventListener('click', function () {
        isRecording = !isRecording;
        if (isRecording) {
          micBtn.classList.add('animate-pulse', 'bg-red-600');
        } else {
          micBtn.classList.remove('animate-pulse', 'bg-red-600');
        }
      });
    }

    const inputField = document.getElementById('chat-input');
    const quickChips = document.querySelectorAll('.quick-chip');
    quickChips.forEach(chip => {
      chip.addEventListener('click', function () {
        if (inputField) {
          inputField.value = this.innerText.replace(/[\u{1F300}-\u{1F9FF}]/gu, '').trim();
          inputField.focus();
        }
      });
    });
  })();
</script></main><nav class="fixed bottom-0 inset-x-0 z-50 pb-safe bg-[#1C0F22]/85 backdrop-blur-xl shadow-[0_-4px_24px_rgba(19,8,24,0.45)]" data-active-classes="text-[#EF5B3D] font-bold"><div class="flex justify-around items-center h-20 px-2"><a class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1 w-14 h-14 rounded-xl text-[#FFF8F0]/60 transition-all" data-path="home" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[24px]">home</span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm">خانه</span></a><a class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1 w-14 h-14 rounded-xl text-[#FFF8F0]/60 transition-all" data-path="my-trips" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[24px]">luggage</span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm">سفرهای من</span></a><a aria-current="page" class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1 w-14 h-14 rounded-xl transition-all text-[#EF5B3D] font-bold" data-path="assistant" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[24px]">smart_toy</span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm">دستیار</span></a><a class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1 w-14 h-14 rounded-xl text-[#FFF8F0]/60 transition-all" data-path="wallet" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[24px]">account_balance_wallet</span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm">کیف پول</span></a><a class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1 w-14 h-14 rounded-xl text-[#FFF8F0]/60 transition-all" data-path="profile" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[24px]">person</span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm">پروفایل</span></a></div></nav></body></html>

<script>
  function switchTripTab(button, tabName) {
    const buttons = document.querySelectorAll('#tripTabs .tab-btn');
    buttons.forEach(btn => {
      btn.className = 'tab-btn flex-1 py-2 px-3 rounded-full font-label-md text-label-md font-medium text-[#CBBDB3] hover:text-[#FFF8F0] transition-all duration-300 flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5';
      const badge = btn.querySelector('span:last-child');
      if (badge) {
        badge.className = 'w-5 h-5 rounded-full bg-[#2F1C37] text-[#CBBDB3] flex items-center justify-center font-label-sm text-label-sm';
      }
    });

    button.className = 'tab-btn flex-1 py-2 px-3 rounded-full font-label-md text-label-md font-bold transition-all duration-300 bg-[#EF5B3D] text-[#FFF8F0] shadow-[0_4px_16px_rgba(239,91,61,0.35)] flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5';
    const activeBadge = button.querySelector('span:last-child');
    if (activeBadge) {
      activeBadge.className = 'w-5 h-5 rounded-full bg-[#FFF8F0]/20 text-[#FFF8F0] flex items-center justify-center font-label-sm text-label-sm';
    }
  }
</script></main><nav class="fixed bottom-0 inset-x-0 z-50 pb-safe bg-[#1C0F22]/85 backdrop-blur-xl shadow-[0_-4px_24px_rgba(19,8,24,0.45)]" data-active-classes="text-[#EF5B3D] font-bold"><div class="flex justify-around items-center h-20 px-2"><a class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1 w-14 h-14 rounded-xl text-[#FFF8F0]/60 transition-all" data-path="home" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[24px]">home</span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm">خانه</span></a><a aria-current="page" class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1 w-14 h-14 rounded-xl transition-all text-[#EF5B3D] font-bold" data-path="my-trips" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[24px]">luggage</span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm">سفرهای من</span></a><a class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1 w-14 h-14 rounded-xl text-[#FFF8F0]/60 transition-all" data-path="assistant" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[24px]">smart_toy</span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm">دستیار</span></a><a class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1 w-14 h-14 rounded-xl text-[#FFF8F0]/60 transition-all" data-path="wallet" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[24px]">account_balance_wallet</span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm">کیف پول</span></a><a class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1 w-14 h-14 rounded-xl text-[#FFF8F0]/60 transition-all" data-path="profile" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[24px]">person</span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm">پروفایل</span></a></div></nav></body></html>

<script>
  function toggleSwitch(btn) {
    const knob = btn.firstElementChild;
    const isChecked = btn.classList.contains('bg-[#EF5B3D]');
    if (isChecked) {
      btn.classList.remove('bg-[#EF5B3D]');
      btn.classList.add('bg-[#382142]');
      knob.classList.remove('-translate-x-6');
      knob.classList.add('translate-x-0');
    } else {
      btn.classList.remove('bg-[#382142]');
      btn.classList.add('bg-[#EF5B3D]');
      knob.classList.remove('translate-x-0');
      knob.classList.add('-translate-x-6');
    }
  }

  function handleLogout() {
    if (confirm('آیا قصد خروج از حساب کاربری خود را دارید؟')) {
      alert('با موفقیت خارج شدید.');
    }
  }
</script></main><nav class="fixed bottom-0 inset-x-0 z-50 pb-safe bg-[#1C0F22]/85 backdrop-blur-xl shadow-[0_-4px_24px_rgba(19,8,24,0.45)]" data-active-classes="text-[#EF5B3D] font-bold"><div class="flex justify-around items-center h-20 px-2"><a class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1 w-14 h-14 rounded-xl text-[#FFF8F0]/60 transition-all" data-path="home" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[24px]">home</span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm">خانه</span></a><a class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1 w-14 h-14 rounded-xl text-[#FFF8F0]/60 transition-all" data-path="my-trips" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[24px]">luggage</span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm">سفرهای من</span></a><a class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1 w-14 h-14 rounded-xl text-[#FFF8F0]/60 transition-all" data-path="assistant" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[24px]">smart_toy</span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm">دستیار</span></a><a class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1 w-14 h-14 rounded-xl text-[#FFF8F0]/60 transition-all" data-path="wallet" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[24px]">account_balance_wallet</span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm">کیف پول</span></a><a aria-current="page" class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1 w-14 h-14 rounded-xl transition-all text-[#EF5B3D] font-bold" data-path="profile" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[24px]">person</span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm">پروفایل</span></a></div></nav></body></html>

<script>
  function toggleSwitch(btn) {
    const knob = btn.firstElementChild;
    const isChecked = btn.classList.contains('bg-[#EF5B3D]');
    if (isChecked) {
      btn.classList.remove('bg-[#EF5B3D]');
      btn.classList.add('bg-[#382142]');
      knob.classList.remove('-translate-x-6');
      knob.classList.add('translate-x-0');
    } else {
      btn.classList.remove('bg-[#382142]');
      btn.classList.add('bg-[#EF5B3D]');
      knob.classList.remove('translate-x-0');
      knob.classList.add('-translate-x-6');
    }
  }

  function handleLogout() {
    if (confirm('آیا قصد خروج از حساب کاربری خود را دارید؟')) {
      alert('با موفقیت خارج شدید.');
    }
  }
</script></main><nav class="fixed bottom-0 inset-x-0 z-50 pb-safe bg-[#1C0F22]/85 backdrop-blur-xl shadow-[0_-4px_24px_rgba(19,8,24,0.45)]" data-active-classes="text-[#EF5B3D] font-bold"><div class="flex justify-around items-center h-20 px-2"><a class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1 w-14 h-14 rounded-xl text-[#FFF8F0]/60 transition-all" data-path="home" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[24px]">home</span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm">خانه</span></a><a class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1 w-14 h-14 rounded-xl text-[#FFF8F0]/60 transition-all" data-path="my-trips" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[24px]">luggage</span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm">سفرهای من</span></a><a class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1 w-14 h-14 rounded-xl text-[#FFF8F0]/60 transition-all" data-path="assistant" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[24px]">smart_toy</span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm">دستیار</span></a><a class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1 w-14 h-14 rounded-xl text-[#FFF8F0]/60 transition-all" data-path="wallet" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[24px]">account_balance_wallet</span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm">کیف پول</span></a><a aria-current="page" class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1 w-14 h-14 rounded-xl transition-all text-[#EF5B3D] font-bold" data-path="profile" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[24px]">person</span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm">پروفایل</span></a></div></nav></body></html>

<script>
  function toggleSwitch(btn) {
    const knob = btn.firstElementChild;
    const isChecked = btn.classList.contains('bg-[#EF5B3D]');
    if (isChecked) {
      btn.classList.remove('bg-[#EF5B3D]');
      btn.classList.add('bg-[#382142]');
      knob.classList.remove('-translate-x-6');
      knob.classList.add('translate-x-0');
    } else {
      btn.classList.remove('bg-[#382142]');
      btn.classList.add('bg-[#EF5B3D]');
      knob.classList.remove('translate-x-0');
      knob.classList.add('-translate-x-6');
    }
  }

  function handleLogout() {
    if (confirm('آیا قصد خروج از حساب کاربری خود را دارید؟')) {
      alert('با موفقیت خارج شدید.');
    }
  }
</script></main><nav class="fixed bottom-0 inset-x-0 z-50 pb-safe bg-[#1C0F22]/85 backdrop-blur-xl shadow-[0_-4px_24px_rgba(19,8,24,0.45)]" data-active-classes="text-[#EF5B3D] font-bold"><div class="flex justify-around items-center h-20 px-2"><a class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1 w-14 h-14 rounded-xl text-[#FFF8F0]/60 transition-all" data-path="home" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[24px]">home</span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm">خانه</span></a><a class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1 w-14 h-14 rounded-xl text-[#FFF8F0]/60 transition-all" data-path="my-trips" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[24px]">luggage</span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm">سفرهای من</span></a><a class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1 w-14 h-14 rounded-xl text-[#FFF8F0]/60 transition-all" data-path="assistant" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[24px]">smart_toy</span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm">دستیار</span></a><a class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1 w-14 h-14 rounded-xl text-[#FFF8F0]/60 transition-all" data-path="wallet" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[24px]">account_balance_wallet</span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm">کیف پول</span></a><a aria-current="page" class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1 w-14 h-14 rounded-xl transition-all text-[#EF5B3D] font-bold" data-path="profile" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[24px]">person</span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm">پروفایل</span></a></div></nav></body></html>

<script>
  function toggleSwitch(btn) {
    const knob = btn.firstElementChild;
    const isChecked = btn.classList.contains('bg-[#EF5B3D]');
    if (isChecked) {
      btn.classList.remove('bg-[#EF5B3D]');
      btn.classList.add('bg-[#382142]');
      knob.classList.remove('-translate-x-6');
      knob.classList.add('translate-x-0');
    } else {
      btn.classList.remove('bg-[#382142]');
      btn.classList.add('bg-[#EF5B3D]');
      knob.classList.remove('translate-x-0');
      knob.classList.add('-translate-x-6');
    }
  }

  function handleLogout() {
    if (confirm('آیا قصد خروج از حساب کاربری خود را دارید؟')) {
      alert('با موفقیت خارج شدید.');
    }
  }
</script></main><nav class="fixed bottom-0 inset-x-0 z-50 pb-safe bg-[#1C0F22]/85 backdrop-blur-xl shadow-[0_-4px_24px_rgba(19,8,24,0.45)]" data-active-classes="text-[#EF5B3D] font-bold"><div class="flex justify-around items-center h-20 px-2"><a class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1 w-14 h-14 rounded-xl text-[#FFF8F0]/60 transition-all" data-path="home" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[24px]">home</span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm">خانه</span></a><a class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1 w-14 h-14 rounded-xl text-[#FFF8F0]/60 transition-all" data-path="my-trips" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[24px]">luggage</span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm">سفرهای من</span></a><a class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1 w-14 h-14 rounded-xl text-[#FFF8F0]/60 transition-all" data-path="assistant" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[24px]">smart_toy</span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm">دستیار</span></a><a class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1 w-14 h-14 rounded-xl text-[#FFF8F0]/60 transition-all" data-path="wallet" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[24px]">account_balance_wallet</span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm">کیف پول</span></a><a aria-current="page" class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1 w-14 h-14 rounded-xl transition-all text-[#EF5B3D] font-bold" data-path="profile" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[24px]">person</span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm">پروفایل</span></a></div></nav></body></html>

<script>
  function toggleSwitch(btn) {
    const knob = btn.firstElementChild;
    const isChecked = btn.classList.contains('bg-[#EF5B3D]');
    if (isChecked) {
      btn.classList.remove('bg-[#EF5B3D]');
      btn.classList.add('bg-[#382142]');
      knob.classList.remove('-translate-x-6');
      knob.classList.add('translate-x-0');
    } else {
      btn.classList.remove('bg-[#382142]');
      btn.classList.add('bg-[#EF5B3D]');
      knob.classList.remove('translate-x-0');
      knob.classList.add('-translate-x-6');
    }
  }

  function handleLogout() {
    if (confirm('آیا قصد خروج از حساب کاربری خود را دارید؟')) {
      alert('با موفقیت خارج شدید.');
    }
  }
</script></main><nav class="fixed bottom-0 inset-x-0 z-50 pb-safe bg-[#1C0F22]/85 backdrop-blur-xl shadow-[0_-4px_24px_rgba(19,8,24,0.45)]" data-active-classes="text-[#EF5B3D] font-bold"><div class="flex justify-around items-center h-20 px-2"><a class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1 w-14 h-14 rounded-xl text-[#FFF8F0]/60 transition-all" data-path="home" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[24px]">home</span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm">خانه</span></a><a class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1 w-14 h-14 rounded-xl text-[#FFF8F0]/60 transition-all" data-path="my-trips" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[24px]">luggage</span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm">سفرهای من</span></a><a class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1 w-14 h-14 rounded-xl text-[#FFF8F0]/60 transition-all" data-path="assistant" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[24px]">smart_toy</span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm">دستیار</span></a><a class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1 w-14 h-14 rounded-xl text-[#FFF8F0]/60 transition-all" data-path="wallet" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[24px]">account_balance_wallet</span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm">کیف پول</span></a><a aria-current="page" class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1 w-14 h-14 rounded-xl transition-all text-[#EF5B3D] font-bold" data-path="profile" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[24px]">person</span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm">پروفایل</span></a></div></nav></body></html>

<script>
  // Simple micro-interaction for toggling tooltip or switching active passengers
  const p1 = document.getElementById('pax-1-btn');
  const p2 = document.getElementById('pax-2-btn');
  const tooltip = document.getElementById('seat-tooltip');

  if (p1 && p2 && tooltip) {
    p1.addEventListener('click', () => {
      p1.classList.add('ring-2', 'ring-primary', 'bg-surface-container-lowest');
      p1.classList.remove('bg-surface-container-high');
      p2.classList.remove('ring-2', 'ring-primary');
      p2.classList.add('bg-surface-container-high');
      tooltip.style.opacity = '1';
      tooltip.style.transform = 'translateY(0)';
    });

    p2.addEventListener('click', () => {
      p2.classList.add('ring-2', 'ring-primary', 'bg-surface-container-lowest');
      p2.classList.remove('bg-surface-container-high');
      p1.classList.remove('ring-2', 'ring-primary');
      p1.classList.add('bg-surface-container-high');
      tooltip.style.opacity = '0.9';
    });
  }
</script></main></body></html>

<script>
  // Simple Countdown Timer for Flight Seat Lock
  let duration = 9 * 60 + 45; // 09:45 in seconds
  const timerDisplay = document.getElementById('fareTimer');
  
  function updateCountdown() {
    const minutes = Math.floor(duration / 60);
    const seconds = duration % 60;
    const formattedMinutes = minutes < 10 ? '۰' + minutes : minutes.toLocaleString('fa-IR');
    const formattedSeconds = seconds < 10 ? '۰' + seconds : seconds.toLocaleString('fa-IR');
    
    if (timerDisplay) {
      timerDisplay.textContent = formattedMinutes + ':' + formattedSeconds;
    }
    
    if (duration > 0) {
      duration--;
    }
  }
  setInterval(updateCountdown, 1000);

  // Accordion Toggle for Breakdown
  const toggleBtn = document.getElementById('toggleSummaryBtn');
  const summaryContent = document.getElementById('summaryContent');
  const arrow = document.getElementById('summaryArrow');

  if (toggleBtn && summaryContent) {
    toggleBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      summaryContent.classList.toggle('hidden');
      if (summaryContent.classList.contains('hidden')) {
        arrow.style.transform = 'rotate(180deg)';
      } else {
        arrow.style.transform = 'rotate(0deg)';
      }
    });
  }

  // Payment Selection Logic
  function selectPayment(type, cardEl) {
    document.querySelectorAll('.payment-card').forEach(card => {
      const radio = card.querySelector('.radio-indicator');
      const innerDot = radio ? radio.querySelector('span') : null;
      if (radio && innerDot) {
        radio.className = 'radio-indicator w-5 h-5 rounded-full bg-surface-container-highest flex items-center justify-center';
        innerDot.className = 'w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-transparent';
      }
    });

    const activeRadio = cardEl.querySelector('.radio-indicator');
    const activeDot = activeRadio ? activeRadio.querySelector('span') : null;
    if (activeRadio && activeDot) {
      activeRadio.className = 'radio-indicator w-5 h-5 rounded-full bg-primary flex items-center justify-center';
      activeDot.className = 'w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-on-primary';
    }
  }

  // Promo Code Simulation
  const applyPromoBtn = document.getElementById('applyPromoBtn');
  const promoFeedback = document.getElementById('promoFeedback');
  if (applyPromoBtn && promoFeedback) {
    applyPromoBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      promoFeedback.classList.remove('hidden');
      applyPromoBtn.textContent = 'ثبت شد';
      setTimeout(() => {
        applyPromoBtn.textContent = 'اعمال مجدد';
      }, 2000);
    });
  }

  // Pay button action feedback
  const payBtn = document.getElementById('payButton');
  if (payBtn) {
    payBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      payBtn.disabled = true;
      payBtn.innerHTML = `
        <span class="inline-block w-4 h-4 border-2 border-on-primary border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin"></span>
        <span>در حال انتقال به درگاه شاپرک...</span>
      `;
      setTimeout(() => {
        payBtn.innerHTML = `
          <span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[20px]">check_circle</span>
          <span>اتصال امن برقرار شد</span>
        `;
      }, 1500);
    });
  }
</script></main></body></html>

<script>
  // Simple Countdown Timer for Flight Seat Lock
  let duration = 9 * 60 + 45; // 09:45 in seconds
  const timerDisplay = document.getElementById('fareTimer');
  
  function updateCountdown() {
    const minutes = Math.floor(duration / 60);
    const seconds = duration % 60;
    const formattedMinutes = minutes < 10 ? '۰' + minutes : minutes.toLocaleString('fa-IR');
    const formattedSeconds = seconds < 10 ? '۰' + seconds : seconds.toLocaleString('fa-IR');
    
    if (timerDisplay) {
      timerDisplay.textContent = formattedMinutes + ':' + formattedSeconds;
    }
    
    if (duration > 0) {
      duration--;
    }
  }
  setInterval(updateCountdown, 1000);

  // Accordion Toggle for Breakdown
  const toggleBtn = document.getElementById('toggleSummaryBtn');
  const summaryContent = document.getElementById('summaryContent');
  const arrow = document.getElementById('summaryArrow');

  if (toggleBtn && summaryContent) {
    toggleBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      summaryContent.classList.toggle('hidden');
      if (summaryContent.classList.contains('hidden')) {
        arrow.style.transform = 'rotate(180deg)';
      } else {
        arrow.style.transform = 'rotate(0deg)';
      }
    });
  }

  // Payment Selection Logic
  function selectPayment(type, cardEl) {
    document.querySelectorAll('.payment-card').forEach(card => {
      const radio = card.querySelector('.radio-indicator');
      const innerDot = radio ? radio.querySelector('span') : null;
      if (radio && innerDot) {
        radio.className = 'radio-indicator w-5 h-5 rounded-full bg-surface-container-highest flex items-center justify-center';
        innerDot.className = 'w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-transparent';
      }
    });

    const activeRadio = cardEl.querySelector('.radio-indicator');
    const activeDot = activeRadio ? activeRadio.querySelector('span') : null;
    if (activeRadio && activeDot) {
      activeRadio.className = 'radio-indicator w-5 h-5 rounded-full bg-primary flex items-center justify-center';
      activeDot.className = 'w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-on-primary';
    }
  }

  // Promo Code Simulation
  const applyPromoBtn = document.getElementById('applyPromoBtn');
  const promoFeedback = document.getElementById('promoFeedback');
  if (applyPromoBtn && promoFeedback) {
    applyPromoBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      promoFeedback.classList.remove('hidden');
      applyPromoBtn.textContent = 'ثبت شد';
      setTimeout(() => {
        applyPromoBtn.textContent = 'اعمال مجدد';
      }, 2000);
    });
  }

  // Pay button action feedback
  const payBtn = document.getElementById('payButton');
  if (payBtn) {
    payBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      payBtn.disabled = true;
      payBtn.innerHTML = `
        <span class="inline-block w-4 h-4 border-2 border-on-primary border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin"></span>
        <span>در حال انتقال به درگاه شاپرک...</span>
      `;
      setTimeout(() => {
        payBtn.innerHTML = `
          <span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[20px]">check_circle</span>
          <span>اتصال امن برقرار شد</span>
        `;
      }, 1500);
    });
  }
</script></main></body></html>

<script>
  // Simple Countdown Timer for Flight Seat Lock
  let duration = 9 * 60 + 45; // 09:45 in seconds
  const timerDisplay = document.getElementById('fareTimer');
  
  function updateCountdown() {
    const minutes = Math.floor(duration / 60);
    const seconds = duration % 60;
    const formattedMinutes = minutes < 10 ? '۰' + minutes : minutes.toLocaleString('fa-IR');
    const formattedSeconds = seconds < 10 ? '۰' + seconds : seconds.toLocaleString('fa-IR');
    
    if (timerDisplay) {
      timerDisplay.textContent = formattedMinutes + ':' + formattedSeconds;
    }
    
    if (duration > 0) {
      duration--;
    }
  }
  setInterval(updateCountdown, 1000);

  // Accordion Toggle for Breakdown
  const toggleBtn = document.getElementById('toggleSummaryBtn');
  const summaryContent = document.getElementById('summaryContent');
  const arrow = document.getElementById('summaryArrow');

  if (toggleBtn && summaryContent) {
    toggleBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      summaryContent.classList.toggle('hidden');
      if (summaryContent.classList.contains('hidden')) {
        arrow.style.transform = 'rotate(180deg)';
      } else {
        arrow.style.transform = 'rotate(0deg)';
      }
    });
  }

  // Payment Selection Logic
  function selectPayment(type, cardEl) {
    document.querySelectorAll('.payment-card').forEach(card => {
      const radio = card.querySelector('.radio-indicator');
      const innerDot = radio ? radio.querySelector('span') : null;
      if (radio && innerDot) {
        radio.className = 'radio-indicator w-5 h-5 rounded-full bg-surface-container-highest flex items-center justify-center';
        innerDot.className = 'w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-transparent';
      }
    });

    const activeRadio = cardEl.querySelector('.radio-indicator');
    const activeDot = activeRadio ? activeRadio.querySelector('span') : null;
    if (activeRadio && activeDot) {
      activeRadio.className = 'radio-indicator w-5 h-5 rounded-full bg-primary flex items-center justify-center';
      activeDot.className = 'w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-on-primary';
    }
  }

  // Promo Code Simulation
  const applyPromoBtn = document.getElementById('applyPromoBtn');
  const promoFeedback = document.getElementById('promoFeedback');
  if (applyPromoBtn && promoFeedback) {
    applyPromoBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      promoFeedback.classList.remove('hidden');
      applyPromoBtn.textContent = 'ثبت شد';
      setTimeout(() => {
        applyPromoBtn.textContent = 'اعمال مجدد';
      }, 2000);
    });
  }

  // Pay button action feedback
  const payBtn = document.getElementById('payButton');
  if (payBtn) {
    payBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      payBtn.disabled = true;
      payBtn.innerHTML = `
        <span class="inline-block w-4 h-4 border-2 border-on-primary border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin"></span>
        <span>در حال انتقال به درگاه شاپرک...</span>
      `;
      setTimeout(() => {
        payBtn.innerHTML = `
          <span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[20px]">check_circle</span>
          <span>اتصال امن برقرار شد</span>
        `;
      }, 1500);
    });
  }
</script></main></body></html>

<script>
  // Simple Countdown Timer for Flight Seat Lock
  let duration = 9 * 60 + 45; // 09:45 in seconds
  const timerDisplay = document.getElementById('fareTimer');
  
  function updateCountdown() {
    const minutes = Math.floor(duration / 60);
    const seconds = duration % 60;
    const formattedMinutes = minutes < 10 ? '۰' + minutes : minutes.toLocaleString('fa-IR');
    const formattedSeconds = seconds < 10 ? '۰' + seconds : seconds.toLocaleString('fa-IR');
    
    if (timerDisplay) {
      timerDisplay.textContent = formattedMinutes + ':' + formattedSeconds;
    }
    
    if (duration > 0) {
      duration--;
    }
  }
  setInterval(updateCountdown, 1000);

  // Accordion Toggle for Breakdown
  const toggleBtn = document.getElementById('toggleSummaryBtn');
  const summaryContent = document.getElementById('summaryContent');
  const arrow = document.getElementById('summaryArrow');

  if (toggleBtn && summaryContent) {
    toggleBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      summaryContent.classList.toggle('hidden');
      if (summaryContent.classList.contains('hidden')) {
        arrow.style.transform = 'rotate(180deg)';
      } else {
        arrow.style.transform = 'rotate(0deg)';
      }
    });
  }

  // Payment Selection Logic
  function selectPayment(type, cardEl) {
    document.querySelectorAll('.payment-card').forEach(card => {
      const radio = card.querySelector('.radio-indicator');
      const innerDot = radio ? radio.querySelector('span') : null;
      if (radio && innerDot) {
        radio.className = 'radio-indicator w-5 h-5 rounded-full bg-surface-container-highest flex items-center justify-center';
        innerDot.className = 'w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-transparent';
      }
    });

    const activeRadio = cardEl.querySelector('.radio-indicator');
    const activeDot = activeRadio ? activeRadio.querySelector('span') : null;
    if (activeRadio && activeDot) {
      activeRadio.className = 'radio-indicator w-5 h-5 rounded-full bg-primary flex items-center justify-center';
      activeDot.className = 'w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-on-primary';
    }
  }

  // Promo Code Simulation
  const applyPromoBtn = document.getElementById('applyPromoBtn');
  const promoFeedback = document.getElementById('promoFeedback');
  if (applyPromoBtn && promoFeedback) {
    applyPromoBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      promoFeedback.classList.remove('hidden');
      applyPromoBtn.textContent = 'ثبت شد';
      setTimeout(() => {
        applyPromoBtn.textContent = 'اعمال مجدد';
      }, 2000);
    });
  }

  // Pay button action feedback
  const payBtn = document.getElementById('payButton');
  if (payBtn) {
    payBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      payBtn.disabled = true;
      payBtn.innerHTML = `
        <span class="inline-block w-4 h-4 border-2 border-on-primary border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin"></span>
        <span>در حال انتقال به درگاه شاپرک...</span>
      `;
      setTimeout(() => {
        payBtn.innerHTML = `
          <span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[20px]">check_circle</span>
          <span>اتصال امن برقرار شد</span>
        `;
      }, 1500);
    });
  }
</script></main></body></html>

<script>
  // Simple Countdown Timer for Flight Seat Lock
  let duration = 9 * 60 + 45; // 09:45 in seconds
  const timerDisplay = document.getElementById('fareTimer');
  
  function updateCountdown() {
    const minutes = Math.floor(duration / 60);
    const seconds = duration % 60;
    const formattedMinutes = minutes < 10 ? '۰' + minutes : minutes.toLocaleString('fa-IR');
    const formattedSeconds = seconds < 10 ? '۰' + seconds : seconds.toLocaleString('fa-IR');
    
    if (timerDisplay) {
      timerDisplay.textContent = formattedMinutes + ':' + formattedSeconds;
    }
    
    if (duration > 0) {
      duration--;
    }
  }
  setInterval(updateCountdown, 1000);

  // Accordion Toggle for Breakdown
  const toggleBtn = document.getElementById('toggleSummaryBtn');
  const summaryContent = document.getElementById('summaryContent');
  const arrow = document.getElementById('summaryArrow');

  if (toggleBtn && summaryContent) {
    toggleBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      summaryContent.classList.toggle('hidden');
      if (summaryContent.classList.contains('hidden')) {
        arrow.style.transform = 'rotate(180deg)';
      } else {
        arrow.style.transform = 'rotate(0deg)';
      }
    });
  }

  // Payment Selection Logic
  function selectPayment(type, cardEl) {
    document.querySelectorAll('.payment-card').forEach(card => {
      const radio = card.querySelector('.radio-indicator');
      const innerDot = radio ? radio.querySelector('span') : null;
      if (radio && innerDot) {
        radio.className = 'radio-indicator w-5 h-5 rounded-full bg-surface-container-highest flex items-center justify-center';
        innerDot.className = 'w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-transparent';
      }
    });

    const activeRadio = cardEl.querySelector('.radio-indicator');
    const activeDot = activeRadio ? activeRadio.querySelector('span') : null;
    if (activeRadio && activeDot) {
      activeRadio.className = 'radio-indicator w-5 h-5 rounded-full bg-primary flex items-center justify-center';
      activeDot.className = 'w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-on-primary';
    }
  }

  // Promo Code Simulation
  const applyPromoBtn = document.getElementById('applyPromoBtn');
  const promoFeedback = document.getElementById('promoFeedback');
  if (applyPromoBtn && promoFeedback) {
    applyPromoBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      promoFeedback.classList.remove('hidden');
      applyPromoBtn.textContent = 'ثبت شد';
      setTimeout(() => {
        applyPromoBtn.textContent = 'اعمال مجدد';
      }, 2000);
    });
  }

  // Pay button action feedback
  const payBtn = document.getElementById('payButton');
  if (payBtn) {
    payBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      payBtn.disabled = true;
      payBtn.innerHTML = `
        <span class="inline-block w-4 h-4 border-2 border-on-primary border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin"></span>
        <span>در حال انتقال به درگاه شاپرک...</span>
      `;
      setTimeout(() => {
        payBtn.innerHTML = `
          <span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[20px]">check_circle</span>
          <span>اتصال امن برقرار شد</span>
        `;
      }, 1500);
    });
  }
</script></main></body></html>

<script>
  // Simple micro-interaction for toggling tooltip or switching active passengers
  const p1 = document.getElementById('pax-1-btn');
  const p2 = document.getElementById('pax-2-btn');
  const tooltip = document.getElementById('seat-tooltip');

  if (p1 && p2 && tooltip) {
    p1.addEventListener('click', () => {
      p1.classList.add('ring-2', 'ring-primary', 'bg-surface-container-lowest');
      p1.classList.remove('bg-surface-container-high');
      p2.classList.remove('ring-2', 'ring-primary');
      p2.classList.add('bg-surface-container-high');
      tooltip.style.opacity = '1';
      tooltip.style.transform = 'translateY(0)';
    });

    p2.addEventListener('click', () => {
      p2.classList.add('ring-2', 'ring-primary', 'bg-surface-container-lowest');
      p2.classList.remove('bg-surface-container-high');
      p1.classList.remove('ring-2', 'ring-primary');
      p1.classList.add('bg-surface-container-high');
      tooltip.style.opacity = '0.9';
    });
  }
</script></main></body></html>

<script>
  // Simple micro-interaction for toggling tooltip or switching active passengers
  const p1 = document.getElementById('pax-1-btn');
  const p2 = document.getElementById('pax-2-btn');
  const tooltip = document.getElementById('seat-tooltip');

  if (p1 && p2 && tooltip) {
    p1.addEventListener('click', () => {
      p1.classList.add('ring-2', 'ring-primary', 'bg-surface-container-lowest');
      p1.classList.remove('bg-surface-container-high');
      p2.classList.remove('ring-2', 'ring-primary');
      p2.classList.add('bg-surface-container-high');
      tooltip.style.opacity = '1';
      tooltip.style.transform = 'translateY(0)';
    });

    p2.addEventListener('click', () => {
      p2.classList.add('ring-2', 'ring-primary', 'bg-surface-container-lowest');
      p2.classList.remove('bg-surface-container-high');
      p1.classList.remove('ring-2', 'ring-primary');
      p1.classList.add('bg-surface-container-high');
      tooltip.style.opacity = '0.9';
    });
  }
</script></main></body></html>
